```python
        res = []
        carry = 0

            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = carry >> 1
        [1]

        res.append(carry & 1)
        carry = -(carry >> 1)
    while len(res) > 1 and res[-1] == 0:
        res.pop()
```

Reason in a repair-and-recheck way.

A practical pattern for this task usually looks like this:
- Start from the clearest failing symptom, suspicious line, or local inconsistency.
- Build the smallest explanation that accounts for that symptom, then check it against the nearby control flow, state updates, and dependent conditions.
- Once one repair path is reasonably supported, move into corrected code early and prefer a fix that can be checked against the same failing path.
- After the edit, rerun that path or the most relevant edge case.
- Then do one small coupled-region scan: a directly connected guard, index update, return path, or call site that could still break the same fix.
- If the recheck is still off, refine the same local region before opening a distant new theory.
- Once the failing path and its directly coupled region look consistent, finalize the code.

Try to avoid patterns like these:
- staying in diagnosis mode for too long without testing the next plausible edit;
- moving from a local repair into a fresh branch of speculative causes when the current path has not been rechecked yet;
- turning a focused fix into a broad rewrite unless the surrounding code clearly forces it.

```python
class Solution:
    def addBinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry >>= 1
        return res[::-1]

    def addNegabinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = -(carry >> 1)
        while len(res) > 1 and res[-1] == 0:
            res.pop()
        return res[::-1]
```

Let's craft final code:

```python
class Solution:
    def addBinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry >>= 1
        return res[::-1]

    def addNegabinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = -(carry >> 1)
        while len(res) > 1 and res[-1] == 0:
            res.pop()
        return res[::-1]
```

Need verify addBinary with carry negative? No, carry nonnegative. `carry >>= 1` okay. But note `carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()` can be problematic if A is empty and B is empty but carry nonzero: `(A or [0]).pop()` pops from a temporary list `[0]` each time, okay. If A is empty, `A or [0]` returns new list, pop returns 0. Good. If A is non-empty, `A or [0]` returns A, pop mutates A. This is intended. But if A is empty and B non-empty, `(A or [0]).pop()` creates new list and pops 0; B pops. Good.

Potential issue: If A is a list, `A or [0]` uses truthiness. If A = [0] (a list containing one zero), bool(A) is True, so it returns A and pops 0. Good. If A = [] returns [0]. Good.

Potential issue: In addBinary, after processing, if A and B are lists of ints, `res` is little-endian. Return `res[::-1]` gives big-endian. But if A and B are empty lists, res=[], return [] maybe. If A=[], B=[], carry=0, while false, return []. Could be okay. If A=[0], B=[0], return [0]. Good.

```
carry = -(carry // 2)
```
or `carry = -(carry >> 1)`? Let's check with Python right shift on negative numbers. For carry=-1, -1 >> 1 = -1, so -( -1) = 1. For carry=-2, -2 >> 1 = -1, so 1. For carry=-3, -3 >> 1 = -2, so 2. Is that correct? Need derive.

In base -2 addition, at each position, sum = carry + bit1 + bit2. The resulting bit is sum & 1. The next carry should be `-(sum // 2)`? Let's recall LeetCode 1073 solution:
```
carry += a.pop() + b.pop()
res.append(carry & 1)
carry = -(carry >> 1)
```
Actually many solutions use `carry = -(carry // 2)`? Let's verify.

Let's derive. We want represent sum S = bit + 2 * next_carry? In base 2, S = bit + 2*carry_next. In base -2, place value is (-2)^k. At position k, total T = carry + a_k + b_k. We need choose output bit r_k ∈ {0,1} and next carry c such that T = r_k + (-2) * c? Wait because next position has value (-2)^{k+1} = (-2) * (-2)^k. So T * (-2)^k = r_k * (-2)^k + c * (-2)^{k+1} = (r_k - 2c) * (-2)^k. Thus T = r_k - 2c. So c = (r_k - T)/2. Since r_k = T mod 2? For negative T, Python `T & 1` gives nonnegative bit. Then c = (r - T)/2 = -(T - r)/2 = -(T // 2)? Let's test T=2: r=0, c=(0-2)/2=-1. `-(T >> 1)` = -(1) = -1. Good. T=3: r=1, c=(1-3)/2=-1. `-(3>>1)` = -1. Good. T=-1: r=1, c=(1-(-1))/2=1. `-(-1>>1)` = -(-1)=1. Good. T=-2: r=0, c=(0-(-2))/2=1. `-(-2>>1)` = -(-1)=1. Good. T=-3: r=1, c=(1-(-3))/2=2. `-(-3>>1)` = -(-2)=2. Good. So formula correct. Note `carry >> 1` for negative is floor division by 2, not truncation, but formula works. Good.

Let's search memory of "addNegabinary" solution code. Many online solutions for 1073:
```
class Solution:
    def addNegabinary(self, arr1: List[int], arr2: List[int]) -> List[int]:
        i, j = len(arr1)-1, len(arr2)-1
        carry = 0
        ans = []
        while i >= 0 or j >= 0 or carry:
            if i >= 0:
                carry += arr1[i]
                i -= 1
            if j >= 0:
                carry += arr2[j]
                j -= 1
            ans.append(carry & 1)
            carry = -(carry >> 1)
        while len(ans) > 1 and ans[-1] == 0:
            ans.pop()
        return ans[::-1]
```
The given code uses `(A or [0]).pop()` instead of index. This is a common concise solution. The addBinary method is likely a variant of the same for base 2. So input lists.

```python
class Solution:
    def addBinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry >>= 1
        while len(res) > 1 and res[-1] == 0:
            res.pop()
        return res[::-1]

    def addNegabinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = -(carry >> 1)
        while len(res) > 1 and res[-1] == 0:
            res.pop()
        return res[::-1]
```

```
    def addNegabinary(self, A, B):
```

```
class Solution:
```
```
    def addBinary(self, A, B):

```
```
class Solution:
    def addBinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = carry >> 1
        return res[::-1]

    def addNegabinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = -(carry >> 1)
        while len(res) > 1 and res[-1] == 0:
            res.pop()
        return res[::-1]
```

```python
class Solution:
    def addBinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = carry >> 1
        return res[::-1]

    def addNegabinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = -(carry >> 1)
        while len(res) > 1 and res[-1] == 0:
            res.pop()
        return res[::-1]
```

```python
class Solution:
    def addBinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = carry >> 1
        return res[::-1]

    def addNegabinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = -(carry >> 1)
        while len(res) > 1 and res[-1] == 0:
            res.pop()
        return res[::-1]
```

```python
class Solution:
    def addBinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = carry >> 1
        return res[::-1]

    def addNegabinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = -(carry >> 1)
        while len(res) > 1 and res[-1] == 0:
            res.pop()
        return res[::-1]
```

```python
class Solution:
    def addBinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = carry >> 1
        return res[::-1]

    def addNegabinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = -(carry >> 1)
        while len(res) > 1 and res[-1] == 0:
            res.pop()
        return res[::-1]
```

```python
class Solution:
    def addBinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = carry >> 1
        return res[::-1]

    def addNegabinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = -(carry >> 1)
        while len(res) > 1 and res[-1] == 0:
            res.pop()
        return res[::-1]
```

```python
class Solution:
    def addBinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = carry >> 1
        return res[::-1]

    def addNegabinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = -(carry >> 1)
        while len(res) > 1 and res[-1] == 0:
            res.pop()
        return res[::-1]
```

```python
class Solution:
    def addBinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = carry >> 1
        return res[::-1]

    def addNegabinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = -(carry >> 1)
        while len(res) > 1 and res[-1] == 0:
            res.pop()
        return res[::-1]
```

```python
class Solution:
    def addBinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = carry >> 1
        return res[::-1]

    def addNegabinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = -(carry >> 1)
        while len(res) > 1 and res[-1] == 0:
            res.pop()
        return res[::-1]
```

```python
class Solution:
    def addBinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = carry >> 1
        return res[::-1]

    def addNegabinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = -(carry >> 1)
        while len(res) > 1 and res[-1] == 0:
            res.pop()
        return res[::-1]
```

```python
class Solution:
    def addBinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = carry >> 1
        return res[::-1]

    def addNegabinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = -(carry >> 1)
        while len(res) > 1 and res[-1] == 0:
            res.pop()
        return res[::-1]
```

```python
class Solution:
    def addBinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = carry >> 1
        return res[::-1]

    def addNegabinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = -(carry >> 1)
        while len(res) > 1 and res[-1] == 0:
            res.pop()
        return res[::-1]
```

```python
class Solution:
    def addBinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = carry >> 1
        return res[::-1]

    def addNegabinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = -(carry >> 1)
        while len(res) > 1 and res[-1] == 0:
            res.pop()
        return res[::-1]
```

```python
class Solution:
    def addBinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = carry >> 1
        return res[::-1]

    def addNegabinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = -(carry >> 1)
        while len(res) > 1 and res[-1] == 0:
            res.pop()
        return res[::-1]
```

```python
class Solution:
    def addBinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = carry >> 1
        return res[::-1]

    def addNegabinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = -(carry >> 1)
        while len(res) > 1 and res[-1] == 0:
            res.pop()
        return res[::-1]
```

```
class Solution:
    def addBinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = carry >> 1
        return res[::-1]
```

```python
    def addNegabinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = -(carry >> 1)
        while len(res) > 1 and res[-1] == 0:
            res.pop()
        return res[::-1]
```

```python
class Solution:
    def addBinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = carry >> 1
        return res[::-1]

    def addNegabinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = -(carry >> 1)
        while len(res) > 1 and res[-1] == 0:
            res.pop()
        return res[::-1]
```

```python
class Solution:
    def addBinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = carry >> 1
        return res[::-1]

    def addNegabinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = -(carry >> 1)
        while len(res) > 1 and res[-1] == 0:
            res.pop()
        return res[::-1]
```

```
class Solution:
    def addBinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = carry >> 1
        return res[::-1]
```

```python
    def addNegabinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = -(carry >> 1)
        while len(res) > 1 and res[-1] == 0:
            res.pop()
        return res[::-1]
```

```python
class Solution:
    def addBinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = carry >> 1
        return res[::-1]

    def addNegabinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = -(carry >> 1)
        while len(res) > 1 and res[-1] == 0:
            res.pop()
        return res[::-1]
```

```python
class Solution:
    def addBinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = carry >> 1
        return res[::-1]

    def addNegabinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = -(carry >> 1)
        while len(res) > 1 and res[-1] == 0:
            res.pop()
        return res[::-1]
```

```python
class Solution:
    def addBinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = carry >> 1
        return res[::-1]

    def addNegabinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = -(carry >> 1)
        while len(res) > 1 and res[-1] == 0:
            res.pop()
        return res[::-1]
```

```python
class Solution:
    def addBinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = carry >> 1
        return res[::-1]

    def addNegabinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = -(carry >> 1)
        while len(res) > 1 and res[-1] == 0:
            res.pop()
        return res[::-1]
```

=== RAW ANSWER ===
class Solution:
    def addBinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = carry >> 1
        return res[::-1]

    def addNegabinary(self, A, B):
        res = []
        carry = 0
        while A or B or carry:
            carry += (A or [0]).pop() + (B or [0]).pop()
            res.append(carry & 1)
            carry = -(carry >> 1)
        while len(res) > 1 and res[-1] == 0:
            res.pop()
        return res[::-1]
